Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the combinatorics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 621. The moduli space has dimension at most 976 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to combinatorics theory, we decompose the problem into 621 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 976. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
